/**
 * The reliability verdict: five questions about a run, answered from structured fields only.
 *
 *   task       what was the agent asked to do, what was going to go wrong, what is graded
 *   fault      what failed and where — simulated vs real, which layer
 *   execution  did the failed call execute? (executed / failed / not executed / unknown are
 *              DIFFERENT answers, and each one says where the knowledge came from)
 *   recovery   what the agent did next, what the harness did (retries, workers), sandbox fate,
 *              and — once graded — the ledger-based recovery checks
 *   result     the workspace diff and the independent grade (hidden tests + checks)
 *
 * Sources, and nothing else: `tool.result.data.{outcome,error_class,fault,attempts,sandbox}`,
 * `fault.fired` (origin defaults to `injected` per the schema), `interruption`, `run.resumed`,
 * `episode.sandbox`, `episode.reset`, `run.finished.data` (incl. `error_class`,
 * `evaluation_status`), the RunRecord's `error_class / interruptions / worker_generation / status`,
 * `episode.evaluated` (ledger / checks / tests / score), `workspace.diff`, `llm.call.attempt`, and
 * exit codes. Never the text of `output` / `error`. When a field is absent the answer is
 * `unknown`, and the panel says why. The only static tables applied are the taxonomy's own
 * (src/lib/codes.ts) and the check-id dictionary (src/lib/checks.ts).
 */

import { checkText, isRecoveryCheck } from './checks'
import {
  codeForInjectedKind,
  codeText,
  exitCodeText,
  layerText,
  originPlain,
  originText,
  taxonomyLabel,
} from './codes'
import { firstSentence, plural } from './format'
import { allCalls, argvTokens, callTouches, isTerminal, normalisePath, type ToolCallView, type ViewState } from './reducer'
import type {
  DiffSummary,
  ExecutionOutcome,
  ExecutionSource,
  Fact,
  FollowUp,
  Grading,
  GradingCheck,
  Incident,
  PanelStatus,
  RawRef,
  SandboxSummary,
  Verdict,
  VerdictPanel,
} from './reliability.types'
import type { ErrorClass, ErrorLayer, ErrorOrigin, FaultFired, Interruption, LedgerEntry, Scenario } from './types'

export interface VerdictOptions {
  /** Override "is the run still producing events" (default: `!isTerminal(state.status)`). */
  live?: boolean
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- incidents

/** Everything known about one failure before it is flattened into an `Incident`. */
interface Seed {
  call: ToolCallView | null
  fault: FaultFired | null
  interruption: Interruption | null
  /** `tool.result.data.error_class` (per call) or the run-level class for a run-level seed. */
  errorClass: ErrorClass | null
  runLevel: boolean
}

function sameFault(a: FaultFired, b: FaultFired): boolean {
  return a.step === b.step && a.kind === b.kind && normalisePath(a.path) === normalisePath(b.path)
}

function seedOrder(a: Seed, b: Seed): number {
  const sa = a.call?.seq ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
  const sb = b.call?.seq ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
  if (sa !== sb) return sa - sb
  const ta = a.fault?.step ?? a.interruption?.step ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
  const tb = b.fault?.step ?? b.interruption?.step ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER
  return ta - tb
}

/**
 * Gather the run's failures: every faulted call, every error result, every orphan `fault.fired`,
 * every `interruption`, plus the run-level `error_class` when nothing else carries it. A call is
 * one seed even when the environment reported it three ways (fault.fired + tool.result.fault +
 * tool.result.error_class), and an interruption merges into the call it names.
 */
function collectSeeds(state: ViewState): Seed[] {
  const calls = allCalls(state)
  const seeds: Seed[] = []
  const byToolUse = new Map<string, Seed>()

  for (const c of calls) {
    const isErr = c.result?.isError === true
    if (!isErr && !c.fault) continue
    const seed: Seed = {
      call: c,
      fault: c.fault,
      interruption: null,
      errorClass: c.result?.errorClass ?? null,
      runLevel: false,
    }
    seeds.push(seed)
    byToolUse.set(c.toolUseId, seed)
  }

  for (const f of state.faults) {
    if (calls.some((c) => c.fault && sameFault(c.fault, f))) continue
    const host = seeds.find(
      (s) => s.call && !s.fault && !s.interruption && s.call.result?.isError && callTouches(s.call, f.path),
    )
    if (host) {
      host.fault = f
      continue
    }
    seeds.push({ call: null, fault: f, interruption: null, errorClass: null, runLevel: false })
  }

  for (const it of state.interruptions) {
    const host = it.tool_use_id ? byToolUse.get(it.tool_use_id) : undefined
    if (host) {
      host.interruption = it
      continue
    }
    const call = it.tool_use_id ? (calls.find((c) => c.toolUseId === it.tool_use_id) ?? null) : null
    const seed: Seed = { call, fault: null, interruption: it, errorClass: null, runLevel: false }
    seeds.push(seed)
    if (call) byToolUse.set(call.toolUseId, seed)
  }

  const ec = state.errorClass
  if (ec && (state.status === 'error' || state.status === 'unevaluated' || state.status === 'interrupted')) {
    const covered = seeds.some(
      (s) =>
        (s.errorClass && s.errorClass.layer === ec.layer && s.errorClass.code === ec.code) ||
        (s.interruption && s.interruption.layer === ec.layer && s.interruption.code === ec.code),
    )
    if (!covered) seeds.push({ call: null, fault: null, interruption: null, errorClass: ec, runLevel: true })
  }

  return seeds.sort(seedOrder)
}

function rowTouches(row: LedgerEntry, path: string | null): boolean {
  if (!path) return true
  if (row.path && normalisePath(row.path) === path) return true
  if (row.command) {
    return argvTokens(row.command).some((t) => {
      const n = normalisePath(t)
      return n === path || n.endsWith(`/${path}`)
    })
  }
  return false
}

/**
 * The evaluate-ledger row for a seed. Injected faults match exactly (the row carries the same
 * FaultFired); interruptions match rows the harness marked `interrupted`; anything else matches
 * the k-th non-ok, non-injected row on the same tool and path, where k is this seed's rank among
 * its peers. Returns null rather than guessing when nothing lines up.
 */
function findLedgerRow(
  ledger: LedgerEntry[],
  seed: Seed,
  tool: string | null,
  path: string | null,
  ordinal: number,
): LedgerEntry | null {
  if (ledger.length === 0 || seed.runLevel) return null
  const f = seed.fault
  if (f) {
    const exact = ledger.find(
      (r) => r.fault && r.fault.kind === f.kind && r.fault.step === f.step && normalisePath(r.fault.path) === normalisePath(f.path),
    )
    if (exact) return exact
    const byStep = ledger.find((r) => r.step === f.step && r.outcome !== 'ok' && rowTouches(r, path))
    if (byStep) return byStep
  }
  if (seed.interruption) {
    const rows = ledger.filter((r) => r.interrupted === true && (!tool || r.tool === tool) && rowTouches(r, path))
    return rows[ordinal] ?? rows[0] ?? null
  }
  if (f) return null
  const rows = ledger.filter((r) => r.outcome !== 'ok' && !r.fault && (!tool || r.tool === tool) && rowTouches(r, path))
  return rows[ordinal] ?? null
}

interface Resolution {
  execution: ExecutionOutcome
  source: ExecutionSource
  sideEffectApplied: boolean | null
  /** One sentence for the panel's `plain`. */
  why: string
}

const FS_CODES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(['ENOENT', 'EACCES'])

function fromLedger(row: LedgerEntry): Resolution {
  const where = `ledger row ${row.step}`
  if (row.interrupted) {
    return row.outcome === 'ok'
      ? {
          execution: 'executed',
          source: 'ledger',
          sideEffectApplied: true,
          why: `${where} shows the call completed although the harness never received the response.`,
        }
      : {
          execution: 'failed',
          source: 'ledger',
          sideEffectApplied: false,
          why: `${where} shows the call ran and failed; the harness never received the response.`,
        }
  }
  switch (row.outcome) {
    case 'ack_lost':
      return {
        execution: 'executed',
        source: 'ledger',
        sideEffectApplied: true,
        why: `${where} is ack_lost: the operation executed fully and only the response was withheld.`,
      }
    case 'short_circuit':
      return {
        execution: 'not_executed',
        source: 'ledger',
        sideEffectApplied: false,
        why: `${where} is short_circuit: sandbox-env answered at the boundary and the sandbox never saw the call.`,
      }
    case 'error': {
      const code = row.error_code ?? null
      if (code && !FS_CODES.has(code)) {
        return {
          execution: 'not_executed',
          source: 'ledger',
          sideEffectApplied: false,
          why: `${where} is error ${code}: the call was rejected before the sandbox ran it.`,
        }
      }
      return {
        execution: 'failed',
        source: 'ledger',
        sideEffectApplied: false,
        why: `${where} is error${code ? ` ${code}` : ''}: the sandbox ran the call and it failed.`,
      }
    }
    default:
      return {
        execution: 'executed',
        source: 'ledger',
        sideEffectApplied: true,
        why: `${where} is ok: the call completed on the server side.`,
      }
  }
}

/**
 * Execution answer for one seed, in priority order:
 *   1. `tool.result.data.outcome` when the harness stated it (anything but `unknown`)
 *   2. `error_class` when `outcome_known` (side effect true → executed; a filesystem-layer error
 *      means the sandbox really ran it → failed; otherwise → not executed)
 *   3. the evaluate ledger, which resolves the `unknown` cases after the fact
 *   4. `error_class` with `outcome_known: false` and no ledger yet → unknown
 *   5. a non-error result with a non-zero exit code → failed (exit code)
 *   6. otherwise unknown — and the reason is spelled out (predates classification, no ledger…)
 */
function resolveExecution(seed: Seed, row: LedgerEntry | null, graded: boolean, live: boolean): Resolution {
  const r = seed.call?.result ?? null
  const ec = seed.errorClass

  if (r?.outcome && r.outcome !== 'unknown') {
    const sideEffect =
      r.outcome === 'executed' ? true : r.outcome === 'not_executed' ? false : (ec?.side_effect_applied ?? false)
    return {
      execution: r.outcome,
      source: 'outcome',
      sideEffectApplied: sideEffect,
      why: `the harness reported the call as ${r.outcome.replace('_', ' ')}.`,
    }
  }

  if (ec && ec.outcome_known) {
    if (ec.side_effect_applied === true) {
      return { execution: 'executed', source: 'error_class', sideEffectApplied: true, why: 'the error class records that the side effect applied.' }
    }
    if (ec.layer === 'filesystem') {
      return {
        execution: 'failed',
        source: 'error_class',
        sideEffectApplied: false,
        why: `a ${ec.origin === 'staged' ? 'staged' : 'real'} OS error: the sandbox ran the call and it failed.`,
      }
    }
    return {
      execution: 'not_executed',
      source: 'error_class',
      sideEffectApplied: ec.side_effect_applied ?? (ec.layer === 'model' || ec.layer === 'gym' ? null : false),
      why:
        ec.origin === 'injected'
          ? 'the call was intercepted at the tool boundary; nothing reached the sandbox.'
          : `the ${layerText(ec.layer)} failed before the call could run.`,
    }
  }

  if (row) return fromLedger(row)

  const pending = live && !graded ? ' The ledger, released at evaluate, will settle it.' : graded ? ' No ledger row matched this call.' : ' The ledger was never released for this run.'

  if (ec) {
    return {
      execution: 'unknown',
      source: 'error_class',
      sideEffectApplied: null,
      why: `the outcome was unknown at the time (${ec.label}).${pending}`,
    }
  }
  if (seed.interruption) {
    return {
      execution: 'unknown',
      source: 'none',
      sideEffectApplied: null,
      why: `the worker was interrupted mid-call and no ledger row resolves it.${pending}`,
    }
  }
  if (r && !r.isError && r.exitCode !== null && r.exitCode !== 0) {
    return {
      execution: 'failed',
      source: 'exit_code',
      sideEffectApplied: false,
      why: `${exitCodeText(r.exitCode)} — from the exit code only; the record carries no error class and no ledger row matched.`,
    }
  }
  if (seed.fault || r?.isError) {
    return {
      execution: 'unknown',
      source: 'none',
      sideEffectApplied: null,
      why: `this record predates error classification (no error_class on the result) and no ledger row matched.`,
    }
  }
  return { execution: 'unknown', source: 'none', sideEffectApplied: null, why: 'nothing in the record says how this call ended.' }
}

function followUpsFor(seed: Seed, path: string | null, calls: ToolCallView[]): FollowUp[] {
  if (!seed.call || !path) return []
  const seq = seed.call.seq
  return calls
    .filter((c) => c.seq > seq && callTouches(c, path))
    .map((c) => ({
      step: c.step,
      tool: c.tool,
      toolUseId: c.toolUseId,
      kind: c.read ? 'read' : c.mutating ? 'write' : 'other',
      ok: c.result ? !c.result.isError : null,
    }))
}

function resultRaw(call: ToolCallView): unknown {
  const r = call.result
  if (!r) return { tool_use_id: call.toolUseId, tool: call.tool, result: null }
  return {
    tool_use_id: call.toolUseId,
    tool: call.tool,
    is_error: r.isError,
    exit_code: r.exitCode,
    error_code: r.errorCode,
    outcome: r.outcome,
    attempts: r.attempts,
    sandbox: r.sandbox,
    fault: call.fault,
    error_class: r.errorClass,
  }
}

/** Build every `Incident` for the run, execution resolved, follow-ups attached. */
export function collectIncidents(state: ViewState, opts: { live: boolean } = { live: !isTerminal(state.status) }): Incident[] {
  const calls = allCalls(state)
  const ledger = state.evaluation?.ledger ?? []
  const graded = state.evaluation !== null
  const seeds = collectSeeds(state)
  const ordinals = new Map<string, number>()

  return seeds.map((seed, i) => {
    const { call, fault, interruption } = seed
    const ec = seed.errorClass

    const origin: ErrorOrigin | 'unknown' =
      ec?.origin ?? (interruption ? 'real' : fault ? (fault.origin ?? 'injected') : 'unknown')
    const layer: ErrorLayer | null =
      ec?.layer ?? interruption?.layer ?? fault?.layer ?? (fault ? (origin === 'staged' ? 'filesystem' : 'boundary') : null)
    const kind = ec?.kind ?? fault?.kind ?? null
    const code =
      ec?.code ??
      interruption?.code ??
      call?.result?.errorCode ??
      (origin === 'injected' ? codeForInjectedKind(kind) : null)
    const label = ec?.label ?? (interruption?.label ? interruption.label : null) ?? taxonomyLabel(origin, kind, layer)
    const tool = call?.tool ?? interruption?.tool ?? null
    const path = fault?.path
      ? normalisePath(fault.path)
      : interruption?.path
        ? normalisePath(interruption.path)
        : (call?.path ?? null)
    const step = call?.step ?? interruption?.step ?? fault?.step ?? null

    const peerKey = `${interruption ? 'int' : 'err'}:${tool ?? ''}:${path ?? ''}`
    const ordinal = fault ? 0 : (ordinals.get(peerKey) ?? 0)
    if (!fault) ordinals.set(peerKey, ordinal + 1)
    const row = findLedgerRow(ledger, seed, tool, path, ordinal)
    const res = resolveExecution(seed, row, graded, opts.live)

    const raw: RawRef[] = []
    if (fault) raw.push({ label: `fault.fired · step ${fault.step} · ${fault.kind}`, json: fault })
    if (call) raw.push({ label: `tool.result · ${call.toolUseId}`, json: resultRaw(call) })
    if (interruption) raw.push({ label: `interruption · ${interruption.code}`, json: interruption })
    if (row) raw.push({ label: `ledger · step ${row.step} · ${row.outcome}`, json: row })
    if (seed.runLevel && ec) raw.push({ label: 'run.finished.error_class', json: ec })

    const id = call ? `call:${call.toolUseId}` : interruption ? `interruption:${interruption.tool_use_id ?? interruption.at}` : fault ? `fault:${fault.step}:${fault.kind}:${path ?? ''}` : `run:${i}`

    return {
      id,
      step,
      tool,
      toolUseId: call?.toolUseId ?? interruption?.tool_use_id ?? null,
      path,
      origin,
      layer,
      code,
      label,
      kind,
      planned: interruption ? interruption.planned : null,
      execution: res.execution,
      executionSource: res.source,
      sideEffectApplied: ec?.side_effect_applied ?? res.sideEffectApplied,
      outcomeKnown: ec?.outcome_known ?? interruption?.outcome_known ?? null,
      followUps: followUpsFor(seed, path, calls),
      errorClass: ec,
      interruption,
      attempts: call?.result?.attempts ?? null,
      description: fault?.description ?? null,
      raw,
      // internal, stripped below
      __why: res.why,
      __mutating: call?.mutating ?? null,
      __read: call?.read ?? null,
      __ledgerStep: row?.step ?? null,
    } as Incident & Internal
  })
}

interface Internal {
  __why: string
  __mutating: boolean | null
  __read: boolean | null
  __ledgerStep: number | null
}

function internal(i: Incident): Internal {
  return i as unknown as Internal
}

function strip(i: Incident & Partial<Internal>): Incident {
  const { __why, __mutating, __read, __ledgerStep, ...rest } = i
  void __why
  void __mutating
  void __read
  void __ledgerStep
  return rest
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- helpers

function originTone(origin: ErrorOrigin | 'unknown'): PanelStatus {
  return origin === 'real' ? 'fail' : origin === 'unknown' ? 'unknown' : 'warn'
}

function where(i: Incident): string {
  const parts: string[] = []
  if (i.step !== null) parts.push(`step ${i.step}`)
  if (i.tool) parts.push(i.tool)
  if (i.path) parts.push(i.path)
  return parts.join(' ')
}

function suffix(i: number, n: number): string {
  return n > 1 ? ` #${i + 1}` : ''
}

function verbFor(tool: string | null): string {
  switch (tool) {
    case 'write_file':
      return 'Write'
    case 'run_command':
      return 'Command'
    case 'read_file':
      return 'Read'
    case 'list_dir':
      return 'Listing'
    default:
      return 'Call'
  }
}

function sourceText(s: ExecutionSource): string {
  switch (s) {
    case 'outcome':
      return 'harness-reported outcome'
    case 'error_class':
      return 'error class'
    case 'ledger':
      return 'evaluate ledger'
    case 'exit_code':
      return 'exit code'
    default:
      return 'no source'
  }
}

function executionText(e: ExecutionOutcome): string {
  switch (e) {
    case 'executed':
      return 'executed'
    case 'failed':
      return 'ran and failed'
    case 'not_executed':
      return 'not executed'
    default:
      return 'unknown'
  }
}

function executionTone(i: Incident): PanelStatus {
  switch (i.execution) {
    case 'executed':
      return 'ok'
    case 'unknown':
      return 'unknown'
    default:
      return i.origin === 'real' ? 'fail' : 'warn'
  }
}

function executionHeadline(i: Incident, live: boolean, graded: boolean): string {
  const verb = verbFor(i.tool)
  const confirm = i.executionSource === 'ledger' ? 'ledger confirms' : i.executionSource === 'outcome' ? 'harness reports' : null
  const withConfirm = (s: string) => (confirm ? `${s} (${confirm})` : s)
  const lostAck = i.kind === 'ack_lost' || (i.code === 'ETIMEDOUT' && i.origin === 'injected')
  switch (i.execution) {
    case 'executed':
      if (lostAck) return withConfirm(`${verb} landed, acknowledgement lost`)
      if (i.code === 'EHARNESS') return withConfirm(`${verb} landed, worker was interrupted`)
      if (i.code === 'ETRANSPORT' || (i.code === 'ETIMEDOUT' && i.origin === 'real')) return withConfirm(`${verb} landed, transport failed`)
      return withConfirm(`${verb} landed`)
    case 'not_executed': {
      const tail = confirm ? `, ${confirm}` : ''
      if (i.kind === 'denied_write' || i.code === 'EACCES') return `Nothing was written (denied at the boundary${tail})`
      if (i.kind === 'missing_file' && i.origin === 'injected') return `Nothing ran (answered at the boundary; file still on disk${tail})`
      if (i.code === 'ESANDBOX') return `Nothing ran (sandbox unavailable${tail})`
      if (i.code === 'EINVAL') return `Nothing ran (rejected as invalid${tail})`
      return `Nothing ran (${layerText(i.layer)}${tail})`
    }
    case 'failed':
      if (i.executionSource === 'exit_code') {
        const code = i.raw.length > 0 ? null : null
        void code
        return `${verb} ran and failed (from exit code)`
      }
      return withConfirm(`${verb} ran and failed (${i.origin === 'staged' ? 'staged OS error' : i.origin === 'real' ? 'real OS error' : 'error'})`)
    default: {
      const pending = live && !graded ? ' · ledger pending' : ''
      if (i.code === 'EHARNESS') return `Unknown: the worker was interrupted mid-call${pending}`
      if (i.code === 'ETRANSPORT') return `Unknown: transport failed mid-call${pending}`
      if (lostAck || i.code === 'ETIMEDOUT') return `Unknown: the acknowledgement was lost${pending}`
      if (i.origin === 'unknown') return 'Unknown: this record predates error classification'
      return `Unknown: the outcome was not recorded${pending}`
    }
  }
}

function countBy<T extends string>(items: Incident[], key: (i: Incident) => T): Map<T, number> {
  const m = new Map<T, number>()
  for (const i of items) m.set(key(i), (m.get(key(i)) ?? 0) + 1)
  return m
}

function joinNonZero(parts: [number, string][]): string {
  return parts
    .filter(([n]) => n > 0)
    .map(([n, w]) => `${n} ${w}`)
    .join(', ')
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- panels

function taskPanel(state: ViewState, scenario: Scenario | null, grading: Grading): VerdictPanel {
  const sc = scenario ?? state.scenario
  const headline = sc?.title ?? sc?.id ?? state.scenarioId ?? 'Unknown task'
  const prompt = state.taskPrompt ?? sc?.task_prompt ?? null

  const sentences: string[] = []
  if (prompt) sentences.push(firstSentence(prompt))

  const kinds = sc?.fault_kinds ?? []
  const injectedLabels = kinds.map((k) => taxonomyLabel('injected', k, 'boundary') ?? k)
  const harness = sc?.harness_faults ?? []
  if (sc) {
    const bits: string[] = []
    if (injectedLabels.length > 0) bits.push(`the scenario injects ${injectedLabels.join('; ')}`)
    if (harness.length > 0) {
      bits.push(
        `includes a real harness interruption (${harness.map((h) => `${h.kind.replace('_', ' ')} on ${h.tool} ${h.path}`).join('; ')})`,
      )
    }
    if (bits.length === 0) bits.push('no faults are planned for this scenario')
    const s = bits.join(' and ')
    sentences.push(`${s.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}${s.slice(1)}.`)
  } else {
    sentences.push('The scenario is not on hand, so the planned faults are unknown.')
  }

  const checkDescriptions =
    sc?.checks && sc.checks.length > 0
      ? sc.checks.map((c) => c.description)
      : grading.available
        ? grading.checks.map((c) => c.description)
        : []
  if (checkDescriptions.length > 0) sentences.push(`Graded on: ${checkDescriptions.join('; ')}.`)

  const facts: Fact[] = []
  facts.push({ label: 'scenario', value: sc?.id ?? state.scenarioId ?? 'unknown' })
  if (state.model) facts.push({ label: 'model', value: state.model })
  facts.push({ label: 'step budget', value: String(sc?.max_steps ?? state.maxSteps) })
  facts.push({
    label: 'faults in play',
    value: kinds.length > 0 ? kinds.join(', ') : sc ? 'none injected' : 'unknown',
    tone: kinds.length > 0 ? 'warn' : undefined,
  })
  if (harness.length > 0) {
    facts.push({
      label: 'real interruption planned',
      value: harness.map((h) => `${h.kind} · ${h.tool} ${h.path} (call #${h.nth})`).join('; '),
      tone: 'fail',
      hint: 'A deliberate real failure the harness inflicts on itself (Scenario.harness_faults).',
    })
  }
  if (checkDescriptions.length > 0) facts.push({ label: 'checks', value: plural(checkDescriptions.length, 'grader check') })

  const raw: RawRef[] = []
  if (sc) {
    raw.push({
      label: 'scenario (public fields)',
      json: {
        id: sc.id,
        title: sc.title,
        fault_kinds: sc.fault_kinds,
        harness_faults: sc.harness_faults ?? [],
        checks: sc.checks ?? [],
        faults_public: sc.faults_public ?? [],
        max_steps: sc.max_steps,
      },
    })
  }
  raw.push({ label: 'task_prompt (episode.reset / RunRecord)', json: prompt })

  return {
    key: 'task',
    status: prompt ? 'ok' : 'unknown',
    headline,
    plain: sentences.join(' '),
    facts,
    raw,
  }
}

function faultPanel(incidents: Incident[], live: boolean): VerdictPanel {
  const n = incidents.length
  const byOrigin = countBy(incidents, (i) => i.origin)
  const injected = byOrigin.get('injected') ?? 0
  const staged = byOrigin.get('staged') ?? 0
  const real = byOrigin.get('real') ?? 0
  const unknown = byOrigin.get('unknown') ?? 0
  const realUnplanned = incidents.filter((i) => i.origin === 'real' && i.planned !== true)
  const realPlanned = incidents.filter((i) => i.origin === 'real' && i.planned === true)

  let headline: string
  if (n === 0) headline = 'No failures'
  else if (n === 1) {
    const i = incidents[0]!
    headline = [i.label ?? i.code ?? 'failure', i.step !== null ? `step ${i.step}` : null, i.path].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')
  } else {
    headline = `${n} failures: ${joinNonZero([
      [injected, 'simulated'],
      [staged, 'staged'],
      [real, 'real'],
      [unknown, 'unknown'],
    ])}`
  }

  const status: PanelStatus = realUnplanned.length > 0 ? 'fail' : unknown > 0 ? 'unknown' : n > 0 ? 'warn' : 'ok'

  const sentences: string[] = []
  if (n === 0) {
    sentences.push(live ? 'No failed call, injected fault or interruption so far.' : 'No failed call, injected fault or interruption was recorded.')
  }
  for (const i of realUnplanned) {
    sentences.push(`A real, unplanned failure hit the ${layerText(i.layer)}${i.label ? ` (${i.label})` : ''}${i.step !== null ? ` at step ${i.step}` : ''}.`)
  }
  for (const i of realPlanned) {
    sentences.push(`The scenario deliberately inflicted a real ${layerText(i.layer)} interruption${i.label ? ` (${i.label})` : ''}${i.step !== null ? ` at step ${i.step}` : ''}.`)
  }
  if (injected > 0) {
    sentences.push(
      `${injected === 1 ? 'One simulated fault was' : `${injected} simulated faults were`} intercepted at the tool boundary by the scenario; nothing real broke there.`,
    )
  }
  if (staged > 0) {
    sentences.push(`${staged === 1 ? 'One failure was' : `${staged} failures were`} staged at reset: a genuine OS error the scenario set up on purpose.`)
  }
  if (unknown > 0) {
    sentences.push(
      `${unknown === 1 ? 'One failure carries' : `${unknown} failures carry`} no error class — this record predates error classification, so the origin cannot be determined.`,
    )
  }

  const facts: Fact[] = []
  incidents.forEach((i, idx) => {
    const sfx = suffix(idx, n)
    facts.push({ label: `origin${sfx}`, value: originText(i.origin), tone: originTone(i.origin), hint: originPlain(i.origin) })
    facts.push({ label: `layer${sfx}`, value: layerText(i.layer) })
    if (i.code) facts.push({ label: `code${sfx}`, value: i.code, hint: codeText(i.code)?.plain })
    if (i.path) facts.push({ label: `path${sfx}`, value: i.path })
    if (i.step !== null || i.tool) facts.push({ label: `step${sfx}`, value: where(i) })
    if (i.planned === true) {
      facts.push({ label: `planned${sfx}`, value: 'deliberate real failure', tone: 'warn', hint: "Triggered by the scenario's harness_faults; still a real failure of a real component." })
    }
    if (i.description) facts.push({ label: `environment says${sfx}`, value: i.description })
  })

  return {
    key: 'fault',
    status,
    headline,
    plain: sentences.join(' '),
    facts,
    raw: incidents.flatMap((i) => i.raw),
  }
}

function executionPanel(incidents: Incident[], live: boolean, graded: boolean): VerdictPanel {
  const n = incidents.length
  const by = countBy(incidents, (i) => i.execution)
  const unknownCount = by.get('unknown') ?? 0
  const realFailed = incidents.some((i) => i.execution === 'failed' && i.origin === 'real')

  let headline: string
  if (n === 0) headline = live ? 'Every call so far was acknowledged' : 'Every call was acknowledged'
  else if (n === 1) headline = executionHeadline(incidents[0]!, live, graded)
  else {
    headline = joinNonZero([
      [by.get('executed') ?? 0, 'executed'],
      [by.get('not_executed') ?? 0, 'not executed'],
      [by.get('failed') ?? 0, 'failed'],
      [unknownCount, 'unknown'],
    ]).replace(/, /g, ' · ')
  }

  const status: PanelStatus = realFailed ? 'fail' : unknownCount > 0 ? 'unknown' : 'ok'

  const sentences: string[] = []
  if (n === 0) sentences.push('No failed call to account for: nothing was refused, lost or interrupted.')
  for (const i of incidents) {
    sentences.push(`${where(i) || 'run'}: ${internal(i).__why}`)
  }
  if (unknownCount > 0 && !graded) {
    sentences.push(live ? 'Grading releases the ledger, which resolves unknown outcomes.' : 'Without the ledger these outcomes stay unknown.')
  }

  const facts: Fact[] = []
  incidents.forEach((i, idx) => {
    const sfx = suffix(idx, n)
    facts.push({
      label: `${where(i) || 'run'}${sfx}`,
      value: `${executionText(i.execution)} — via ${sourceText(i.executionSource)}`,
      tone: executionTone(i),
      hint: internal(i).__why,
    })
    if (i.sideEffectApplied !== null) {
      facts.push({ label: `side effect applied${sfx}`, value: i.sideEffectApplied ? 'yes' : 'no' })
    }
    if (i.outcomeKnown !== null) {
      facts.push({
        label: `outcome known at the time${sfx}`,
        value: i.outcomeKnown ? 'yes' : 'no',
        tone: i.outcomeKnown ? undefined : 'warn',
        hint: i.outcomeKnown ? undefined : (codeText(i.code)?.verifyHint ?? 'Verify before retrying.'),
      })
    }
    if (i.attempts !== null && i.attempts !== undefined && i.attempts > 1) {
      facts.push({ label: `harness retries of this call${sfx}`, value: String(i.attempts - 1), tone: 'warn' })
    }
  })

  return {
    key: 'execution',
    status,
    headline,
    plain: sentences.join(' '),
    facts,
    raw: incidents.flatMap((i) => i.raw),
  }
}

interface ReadBack {
  label: string
  value: string
  tone?: PanelStatus
}

/** Live hint from call order only: after a write incident, did a read precede the next write? */
function readBackFor(i: Incident): ReadBack | null {
  const int = internal(i)
  if (!i.path) return null
  const fu = i.followUps
  if (int.__mutating) {
    const firstWrite = fu.findIndex((f) => f.kind === 'write')
    const before = firstWrite === -1 ? fu : fu.slice(0, firstWrite)
    const readOk = before.some((f) => f.kind === 'read' && f.ok !== false)
    if (firstWrite === -1) {
      return readOk
        ? { label: 'read-back before next write', value: 'yes — read back, never rewrote', tone: 'ok' }
        : { label: 'read-back before next write', value: 'n.a. — never touched again', tone: 'unknown' }
    }
    return readOk
      ? { label: 'read-back before next write', value: 'yes', tone: 'ok' }
      : { label: 'read-back before next write', value: 'no — rewrote without reading back', tone: 'fail' }
  }
  if (int.__read) {
    const retried = fu.some((f) => f.kind === 'read' && f.ok === true)
    return retried
      ? { label: 'retried the read', value: 'yes', tone: 'ok' }
      : { label: 'retried the read', value: fu.length === 0 ? 'no — never touched again' : 'no successful re-read', tone: 'fail' }
  }
  return null
}

function recoveryPanel(state: ViewState, incidents: Incident[], grading: Grading, sandbox: SandboxSummary, live: boolean): VerdictPanel {
  const n = incidents.length
  const recChecks = grading.checks.filter((c) => isRecoveryCheck(c.id))
  const failed = recChecks.filter((c) => !c.ok)
  const maxAttempt = state.llmCalls.reduce((m, c) => Math.max(m, c.attempt), 0)

  const facts: Fact[] = []
  const hints: string[] = []
  incidents.forEach((i, idx) => {
    const rb = readBackFor(i)
    if (!rb) return
    hints.push(rb.value.split(' — ')[0]!)
    facts.push({
      label: `${rb.label}${suffix(idx, n)} (live hint)`,
      value: rb.value,
      tone: rb.tone,
      hint: "Derived from call order only; the grader's ledger checks are authoritative.",
    })
    const reads = i.followUps.filter((f) => f.kind === 'read').length
    const writes = i.followUps.filter((f) => f.kind === 'write').length
    if (i.followUps.length > 0) {
      facts.push({ label: `follow-ups on ${i.path}${suffix(idx, n)}`, value: `${plural(reads, 'read')}, ${plural(writes, 'write')}` })
    }
  })
  facts.push({
    label: 'harness attempts',
    value: maxAttempt === 0 ? 'unknown (no llm.call events)' : maxAttempt === 1 ? '1 (no retries)' : `${maxAttempt} (${plural(maxAttempt - 1, 'retried request')})`,
    tone: maxAttempt > 1 ? 'warn' : undefined,
    hint: 'Highest llm.call attempt: model requests retried by the harness after a 429/5xx.',
  })
  facts.push({
    label: 'workers',
    value: `${sandbox.workerGeneration} (${plural(sandbox.resumes, 'resume')})`,
    tone: sandbox.resumes > 0 ? 'warn' : undefined,
    hint: 'worker_generation = 1 + the number of times a fresh worker resumed the run.',
  })
  facts.push({
    label: 'sandbox',
    value:
      sandbox.lost === true
        ? sandbox.lostAtStep !== null
          ? `lost at step ${sandbox.lostAtStep}`
          : 'lost'
        : sandbox.lost === false
          ? 'survived'
          : 'unknown',
    tone: sandbox.lost === true ? 'fail' : sandbox.lost === false ? 'ok' : 'unknown',
    hint:
      sandbox.lost === true
        ? grading.available
          ? 'A sandbox-layer failure was observed; grading still ran afterwards.'
          : 'A sandbox-layer failure was observed and the run was not graded inside it.'
        : sandbox.lost === false
          ? 'Grading ran inside the sandbox, so it was alive at the end.'
          : 'No grading and no sandbox-layer failure recorded: cannot say.',
  })
  for (const c of recChecks) {
    const t = checkText(c.id)
    facts.push({
      label: c.id,
      value: c.ok ? 'ok' : 'failed',
      tone: c.ok ? 'ok' : 'fail',
      hint: c.ok ? t.description : `${t.whenFailed}${c.detail ? ` — ${c.detail}` : ''}`,
    })
  }

  let status: PanelStatus
  let headline: string
  let plain: string
  const hintText = hints.length > 0 ? hints.join(' / ') : 'n.a.'

  if (grading.available && recChecks.length > 0) {
    if (failed.length > 0) {
      status = 'fail'
      headline = `Recovery check failed: ${failed[0]!.id}${failed.length > 1 ? ` +${failed.length - 1}` : ''}`
      plain = failed.map((c) => `${checkText(c.id).whenFailed}${c.detail ? ` (${c.detail})` : ''}.`).join(' ')
    } else {
      status = 'ok'
      headline = `Recovery verified by the grader (${recChecks.length}/${recChecks.length} checks)`
      plain = `The grader's ledger-based checks confirm the agent handled the failure correctly: ${recChecks.map((c) => checkText(c.id).description).join('; ')}.`
    }
  } else if (n === 0) {
    status = live ? 'pending' : 'ok'
    headline = live ? 'No failures to recover from yet' : 'Nothing to recover from'
    plain = live
      ? 'No fault, real error or interruption so far.'
      : 'No fault, real error or interruption was recorded, so there was nothing to recover from.'
  } else if (live && !grading.available) {
    status = 'pending'
    headline = `Awaiting grading · read-back so far: ${hintText}`
    plain = 'The recovery checks are computed from the ledger at evaluate time. Until then the facts here are hints from call order only.'
  } else if (grading.available) {
    status = 'unknown'
    headline = 'Grader did not score recovery for this scenario'
    plain = `The evaluation carries no recovery check, so recovery is unverified. Live hint from call order: read-back ${hintText}.`
  } else {
    status = 'unknown'
    headline = `Not graded${grading.blockedBy ? `: ${grading.blockedBy}` : ''}`
    plain = `The run was never evaluated, so recovery is unverified. Live hint from call order: read-back ${hintText}.`
  }

  const raw: RawRef[] = []
  if (grading.available) raw.push({ label: 'episode.evaluated.checks (recovery)', json: recChecks })
  for (const i of incidents) if (i.followUps.length > 0) raw.push({ label: `follow-ups · ${i.path}`, json: i.followUps })
  raw.push({ label: 'llm.call attempts', json: state.llmCalls.map((c) => ({ step: c.step, attempt: c.attempt, error: c.error })) })
  if (state.resumes.length > 0) raw.push({ label: 'run.resumed', json: state.resumes })
  if (state.interruptions.length > 0) raw.push({ label: 'interruption', json: state.interruptions })
  if (state.sandboxEvents.length > 0) raw.push({ label: 'episode.sandbox', json: state.sandboxEvents })

  return { key: 'recovery', status, headline, plain, facts, raw }
}

function resultPanel(state: ViewState, grading: Grading, diff: DiffSummary | null, live: boolean): VerdictPanel {
  const blocked = state.status === 'unevaluated' || state.status === 'interrupted' || state.status === 'error'
  const okChecks = grading.checks.filter((c) => c.ok).length
  const failedChecks = grading.checks.filter((c) => !c.ok)
  const tests = grading.tests
  const testsTotal = tests ? tests.passed + tests.failed + tests.errors : 0
  const filesText = diff ? `${plural(diff.files, 'file')} changed` : null

  let status: PanelStatus
  let headline: string
  let plain: string

  if (grading.available) {
    const passed = state.evaluation?.passed === true
    status = passed && failedChecks.length === 0 ? 'ok' : 'warn'
    headline = [
      `Score ${Math.round(grading.score ?? 0)}`,
      tests ? `tests ${tests.passed}/${testsTotal}` : null,
      `checks ${okChecks}/${grading.checks.length}`,
      filesText,
    ]
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join(' · ')
    const bits = [
      `Hidden tests ${passed ? 'passed' : 'did not pass'}${tests ? ` (${tests.passed}/${testsTotal})` : ''}`,
      `${okChecks}/${grading.checks.length} grader checks passed${failedChecks.length > 0 ? ` — failed: ${failedChecks.map((c) => c.id).join(', ')}` : ''}`,
    ]
    plain = `${bits.join('; ')}.${diff ? ` ${plural(diff.files, 'file')} changed (+${diff.added} / −${diff.removed}).` : ''}`
  } else if (blocked) {
    status = state.errorClass?.origin === 'real' ? 'fail' : 'unknown'
    headline = grading.blockedBy ? `Not graded: ${grading.blockedBy}` : `Not graded (${state.status}; no error class recorded)`
    const why =
      state.status === 'unevaluated'
        ? "The loop finished but evaluate() failed — not the agent's fault."
        : state.status === 'interrupted'
          ? 'A real interruption ended the run and no worker resumed it.'
          : 'The harness could not run the episode.'
    plain = `Grading did not run: ${grading.blockedBy ?? 'no reason recorded'}. ${why}${diff ? ` ${plural(diff.files, 'file')} had changed.` : ''}`
  } else if (live) {
    status = 'pending'
    headline = `Awaiting grading${filesText ? ` · ${filesText} so far` : ''}`
    plain = 'Grading runs when the agent submits or the step budget ends; hidden tests are only uploaded then.'
  } else {
    status = 'unknown'
    headline = `Not graded${filesText ? ` · ${filesText}` : ''}`
    plain = `The run ended with status ${state.status} but no evaluation was recorded.`
  }

  const facts: Fact[] = []
  if (grading.available) {
    facts.push({ label: 'score', value: String(Math.round(grading.score ?? 0)), tone: status })
    if (tests) {
      facts.push({
        label: 'tests',
        value: `${tests.passed} passed, ${tests.failed} failed, ${tests.errors} errors`,
        tone: tests.failed + tests.errors === 0 && tests.passed > 0 ? 'ok' : 'fail',
      })
    }
    for (const c of grading.checks) {
      facts.push({
        label: c.id,
        value: c.ok ? 'ok' : 'failed',
        tone: c.ok ? 'ok' : 'fail',
        hint: c.ok ? c.description : `${checkText(c.id).whenFailed}${c.detail ? ` — ${c.detail}` : ''}`,
      })
    }
  }
  if (grading.evaluationStatus) facts.push({ label: 'evaluation', value: grading.evaluationStatus })
  if (grading.blockedBy) facts.push({ label: 'blocked by', value: grading.blockedBy, tone: state.errorClass?.origin === 'real' ? 'fail' : 'unknown' })
  if (diff) {
    facts.push({ label: 'files changed', value: diff.files > 0 ? diff.paths.join(', ') : 'none' })
    facts.push({ label: 'lines', value: `+${diff.added} / −${diff.removed}` })
  }

  const raw: RawRef[] = []
  if (state.evaluation) {
    raw.push({
      label: 'episode.evaluated',
      json: {
        score: state.evaluation.score,
        passed: state.evaluation.passed,
        tests: state.evaluation.tests,
        checks: state.evaluation.checks,
      },
    })
  }
  if (state.diffs.length > 0) raw.push({ label: 'workspace.diff (last)', json: state.diffs.map((d) => d.path) })
  if (state.errorClass) raw.push({ label: 'run-level error_class', json: state.errorClass })
  if (state.evaluationStatus || state.evaluationError) {
    raw.push({ label: 'run.finished evaluation', json: { evaluation_status: state.evaluationStatus, evaluation_error: state.evaluationError } })
  }

  return { key: 'result', status, headline, plain, facts, raw }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- summaries

/** `+`/`-` lines of the last workspace.diff, excluding the `+++`/`---` file headers. */
export function summariseDiff(diffs: ViewState['diffs']): DiffSummary | null {
  if (diffs.length === 0) return null
  let added = 0
  let removed = 0
  for (const d of diffs) {
    for (const line of d.unified.split('\n')) {
      if (line.startsWith('+++') || line.startsWith('---')) continue
      if (line.startsWith('+')) added += 1
      else if (line.startsWith('-')) removed += 1
    }
  }
  return { files: diffs.length, added, removed, paths: diffs.map((d) => d.path) }
}

function buildGrading(state: ViewState): Grading {
  const ev = state.evaluation
  const blocked = state.status === 'unevaluated' || state.status === 'interrupted' || state.status === 'error'
  const checks: GradingCheck[] = (ev?.checks ?? []).map((c) => ({
    id: c.id,
    ok: c.ok === true,
    weight: typeof c.weight === 'number' ? c.weight : 1,
    description: checkText(c.id).description,
    detail: c.detail ?? '',
  }))
  const tests = ev?.tests
  return {
    available: ev !== null,
    evaluationStatus: state.evaluationStatus,
    score: ev ? ev.score : null,
    tests: tests ? { passed: tests.passed ?? 0, failed: tests.failed ?? 0, errors: tests.errors ?? 0 } : null,
    checks,
    blockedBy: blocked ? (state.errorClass?.label ?? state.evaluationError ?? null) : null,
  }
}

function buildSandbox(state: ViewState, incidents: Incident[]): SandboxSummary {
  const sandboxIncident = incidents.find((i) => i.layer === 'sandbox') ?? null
  const notice = state.sandboxEvents.find((e) => e.status === 'terminated' || e.status === 'replaced') ?? null
  const lostByRecord = state.errorClass?.layer === 'sandbox'
  const lost = sandboxIncident || notice || lostByRecord ? true : state.evaluation ? false : null
  const resumes = Math.max(state.resumes.length, state.workerGeneration - 1)
  return {
    episodeId: state.episodeId,
    sandboxId: state.sandboxId,
    alive: lost === true ? false : state.sandboxAlive,
    attempt: state.resetAttempt,
    workerGeneration: Math.max(state.workerGeneration, resumes + 1),
    resumes,
    lost,
    lostAtStep: sandboxIncident?.step ?? notice?.step ?? null,
  }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- entry point

/** Pure: the same state and scenario always give the same verdict. */
export function buildVerdict(state: ViewState, scenario: Scenario | null, opts: VerdictOptions = {}): Verdict {
  const live = opts.live ?? !isTerminal(state.status)
  const graded = state.evaluation !== null
  const rich = collectIncidents(state, { live })
  const grading = buildGrading(state)
  const sandbox = buildSandbox(state, rich)
  const diff = summariseDiff(state.diffs)

  const panels = {
    task: taskPanel(state, scenario, grading),
    fault: faultPanel(rich, live),
    execution: executionPanel(rich, live, graded),
    recovery: recoveryPanel(state, rich, grading, sandbox, live),
    result: resultPanel(state, grading, diff, live),
  }

  return {
    status: state.status ?? null,
    live,
    panels,
    incidents: rich.map((i) => strip(i as Incident & Partial<Internal>)),
    sandbox,
    grading,
    diff,
  }
}

/**
 * The reliability verdict is a pure function of structured event fields. These tests pin down
 * (a) the story the real lost-ack capture tells, (b) the new taxonomy end to end (injected EACCES,
 * injected ETIMEDOUT resolved by the ledger, a real ESANDBOX that ends the run), (c) a planned
 * worker crash with a resume, (d) a clean run, and (e) that message text is never consulted.
 */

import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { resolve } from 'node:path'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

import { fromRunRecord, initialState, reduceAll } from './reducer'
import { buildVerdict, collectIncidents, summariseDiff } from './reliability'
import type { ErrorClass, Event, Interruption, LedgerEntry, RunRecord, Scenario } from './types'

const record = JSON.parse(readFileSync(resolve(process.cwd(), 'public/demo/lost-ack.json'), 'utf8')) as RunRecord

let nextId = 0
function ev(type: Event['type'], data: Record<string, unknown>, step?: number | null): Event {
  nextId += 1
  return {
    id: nextId - 1,
    ts: new Date(1_789_491_600_000 + nextId * 1000).toISOString(),
    run_id: 'r_test',
    type,
    step: step ?? null,
    data,
  }
}
function reset() {
  nextId = 0
}
function call(step: number, tool: string, input: Record<string, unknown>, id: string): Event {
  return ev('tool.call', { tool, input, tool_use_id: id }, step)
}
function result(step: number, id: string, tool: string, output: unknown, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}): Event {
  const isError = extra.is_error === true
  return ev(
    'tool.result',
    {
      tool_use_id: id,
      tool,
      output: typeof output === 'string' ? output : JSON.stringify(output),
      is_error: isError,
      duration_ms: 100,
      ...extra,
    },
    step,
  )
}

const EACCES_INJECTED: ErrorClass = {
  origin: 'injected',
  layer: 'boundary',
  code: 'EACCES',
  kind: 'denied_write',
  label: 'simulated: write denied (nothing written)',
  outcome_known: true,
  side_effect_applied: false,
}
const ETIMEDOUT_INJECTED: ErrorClass = {
  origin: 'injected',
  layer: 'boundary',
  code: 'ETIMEDOUT',
  kind: 'ack_lost',
  label: 'simulated: lost ack (write landed; response withheld)',
  outcome_known: false,
  side_effect_applied: null,
}
const ESANDBOX_REAL: ErrorClass = {
  origin: 'real',
  layer: 'sandbox',
  code: 'ESANDBOX',
  kind: null,
  label: 'real: sandbox terminated or unavailable',
  outcome_known: true,
  side_effect_applied: false,
}

const scenario: Scenario = {
  id: 'gauntlet',
  title: 'Gauntlet: all three faults',
  description: '',
  task_prompt: 'Prepare release 0.2.0 of this package. Then run the tests.',
  max_steps: 20,
  fault_kinds: ['denied_write', 'ack_lost'],
  harness_faults: [],
  checks: [
    { id: 'write_eventually_succeeded', description: 'after EACCES on limits.py a later write to it succeeded', weight: 1 },
    { id: 'verified_before_rewrite', description: 'after the timed-out write the agent read CHANGELOG.md before any further write to it', weight: 2 },
  ],
}

describe('(a) the real lost-ack capture (predates the taxonomy)', () => {
  const state = fromRunRecord(record)
  const v = buildVerdict(state, null)

  it('is not live and reports the run status', () => {
    expect(v.live).toBe(false)
    expect(v.status).toBe('ok')
  })

  it('finds exactly one incident: an injected ack_lost on CHANGELOG.md, labelled from the taxonomy table', () => {
    expect(v.incidents).toHaveLength(1)
    const i = v.incidents[0]!
    expect(i.origin).toBe('injected')
    expect(i.kind).toBe('ack_lost')
    expect(i.layer).toBe('boundary')
    expect(i.code).toBe('ETIMEDOUT')
    expect(i.path).toBe('CHANGELOG.md')
    expect(i.tool).toBe('write_file')
    expect(i.step).toBe(4)
    expect(i.label).toBe('simulated: lost ack (write landed; response withheld)')
    expect(i.errorClass).toBeNull()
    expect(i.outcomeKnown).toBeNull()
    expect(i.planned).toBeNull()
    expect(v.panels.fault.status).toBe('warn')
    expect(v.panels.fault.headline).toBe('simulated: lost ack (write landed; response withheld) · step 4 · CHANGELOG.md')
  })

  it('resolves execution through the evaluate ledger: the write landed', () => {
    const i = v.incidents[0]!
    expect(i.execution).toBe('executed')
    expect(i.executionSource).toBe('ledger')
    expect(i.sideEffectApplied).toBe(true)
    expect(v.panels.execution.status).toBe('ok')
    expect(v.panels.execution.headline).toBe('Write landed, acknowledgement lost (ledger confirms)')
    expect(i.raw.some((r) => r.label.startsWith('ledger · step 6 · ack_lost'))).toBe(true)
  })

  it('sees the read-back as the follow-up and the grader confirms recovery', () => {
    const i = v.incidents[0]!
    expect(i.followUps.map((f) => f.kind)).toEqual(['read', 'read'])
    expect(i.followUps.every((f) => f.ok)).toBe(true)
    expect(v.panels.recovery.status).toBe('ok')
    expect(v.panels.recovery.headline).toBe('Recovery verified by the grader (2/2 checks)')
    const rb = v.panels.recovery.facts.find((f) => f.label.startsWith('read-back before next write'))!
    expect(rb.value).toBe('yes — read back, never rewrote')
    expect(v.panels.recovery.facts.find((f) => f.label === 'workers')!.value).toBe('1 (0 resumes)')
    expect(v.panels.recovery.facts.find((f) => f.label === 'sandbox')!.value).toBe('survived')
  })

  it('reports the independent grade and the diff', () => {
    expect(v.grading.available).toBe(true)
    expect(v.grading.score).toBe(100)
    expect(v.grading.tests).toEqual({ passed: 8, failed: 0, errors: 0 })
    expect(v.grading.checks.map((c) => c.id)).toEqual(['verified_before_rewrite', 'no_duplicate_entry', 'version_bumped'])
    expect(v.grading.checks[0]!.description).toContain('read CHANGELOG.md before writing')
    expect(v.grading.blockedBy).toBeNull()
    expect(v.diff).toEqual({ files: 2, added: 5, removed: 1, paths: ['CHANGELOG.md', 'src/ratelimiter/version.py'] })
    expect(v.panels.result.status).toBe('ok')
    expect(v.panels.result.headline).toBe('Score 100 · tests 8/8 · checks 3/3 · 2 files changed')
  })

  it('knows the sandbox survived because grading ran inside it', () => {
    expect(v.sandbox).toMatchObject({ episodeId: 'ep_6099ee548804', workerGeneration: 1, resumes: 0, lost: false, lostAtStep: null })
  })

  it('takes the task from the scenario carried by episode.reset', () => {
    expect(v.panels.task.status).toBe('ok')
    expect(v.panels.task.headline).toBe('Release 0.2.0 when the write ack is lost')
    expect(v.panels.task.plain).toContain('Prepare release 0.2.0 of this package')
    expect(v.panels.task.plain).toContain('injects simulated: lost ack (write landed; response withheld)')
    expect(v.panels.task.plain).toContain('Graded on:')
    expect(v.panels.task.plain).not.toContain('real harness interruption')
  })

  it('is deterministic', () => {
    expect(buildVerdict(state, null)).toEqual(v)
  })
})

    expect(v.grading.evaluationStatus).toBe('ok')

  it('a real ESANDBOX that ends the run as interrupted: fault fail, sandbox lost at that step, result blocked', () => {
    const base: RunRecord = {
      run_id: 'r_b',
      status: 'interrupted',
      scenario_id: 'gauntlet',
      model: 'claude-haiku-4-5',
      max_steps: 20,
      episode_id: 'ep_b',
      created_at: '2026-09-12T17:00:00.000Z',
      finished_at: '2026-09-12T17:01:00.000Z',
      events: [...events(), ev('run.finished', { status: 'interrupted', error_class: ESANDBOX_REAL, evaluation_status: 'skipped' })],
      usage: { input_tokens: 1, output_tokens: 1 },
      error_class: ESANDBOX_REAL,
      interruptions: [],
      worker_generation: 1,
    }
    const s = fromRunRecord(base)
    expect(s.status).toBe('interrupted')
    expect(s.errorClass).toEqual(ESANDBOX_REAL)
    const v = buildVerdict(s, scenario)
    expect(v.live).toBe(false)
    // the run-level class is already represented by the step-5 incident: no duplicate
    expect(v.incidents).toHaveLength(3)
    expect(v.panels.fault.status).toBe('fail')
    expect(v.sandbox.lost).toBe(true)
    expect(v.sandbox.lostAtStep).toBe(5)
    expect(v.sandbox.alive).toBe(false)
    expect(v.grading.available).toBe(false)
    expect(v.grading.blockedBy).toBe('real: sandbox terminated or unavailable')
    expect(v.grading.evaluationStatus).toBe('skipped')
    expect(v.panels.result.status).toBe('fail')
    expect(v.panels.result.headline).toBe('Not graded: real: sandbox terminated or unavailable')
    expect(v.panels.result.plain).toContain('A real interruption ended the run and no worker resumed it.')
    expect(v.panels.recovery.status).toBe('unknown')
    expect(v.panels.recovery.headline).toBe('Not graded: real: sandbox terminated or unavailable')
    expect(v.panels.recovery.facts.find((f) => f.label === 'sandbox')!.value).toBe('lost at step 5')
    // the lost ack never got a ledger: it stays unknown and says why
    const ack = v.incidents[1]!
    expect(ack.execution).toBe('unknown')
    expect(v.panels.execution.status).toBe('unknown')
    expect(v.panels.execution.plain).toContain('The ledger was never released for this run.')
  })

  it('a run-level error class nothing else carries becomes its own incident (evaluate failed: EGYM)', () => {
    reset()
    const s = reduceAll([
      ev('run.started', { scenario_id: 'lost-ack', model: 'm' }),
      call(1, 'read_file', { path: 'README.md' }, 'tu_1'),
      result(1, 'tu_1', 'read_file', { path: 'README.md', content: 'x', size: 1, sha256: 'a' }),
      ev('run.finished', {
        status: 'unevaluated',
        evaluation_status: 'failed',
        evaluation_error: 'evaluate: 503',
        error_class: { origin: 'real', layer: 'gym', code: 'EGYM', label: 'real: gym control-plane failure', outcome_known: true },
      }),
    ])
    const v = buildVerdict(s, null)
    expect(v.incidents).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(v.incidents[0]).toMatchObject({ origin: 'real', layer: 'gym', code: 'EGYM', step: null, tool: null, execution: 'not_executed', executionSource: 'error_class' })
    expect(v.panels.fault.status).toBe('fail')
    expect(v.panels.fault.headline).toBe('real: gym control-plane failure')
    expect(v.panels.result.headline).toBe('Not graded: real: gym control-plane failure')
    expect(v.panels.result.plain).toContain("not the agent's fault")
    expect(v.sandbox.lost).toBeNull()
  })

  it('falls back to evaluation_error for blockedBy when there is no error class', () => {
    reset()
    const s = reduceAll([ev('run.finished', { status: 'unevaluated', evaluation_status: 'failed', evaluation_error: 'evaluate timed out' })])
    const v = buildVerdict(s, null)
    expect(v.grading.blockedBy).toBe('evaluate timed out')
    expect(v.panels.result.status).toBe('unknown')
    expect(v.incidents).toHaveLength(0)
  })
})

describe('(c) worker crash: planned interruption + resume', () => {
  const interruption: Interruption = {
    step: 4,
    layer: 'harness',
    code: 'EHARNESS',
    label: 'real: harness worker interrupted mid-call (outcome unknown)',
    tool_use_id: 'tu_w',
    tool: 'write_file',
    path: 'CHANGELOG.md',
    outcome_known: false,
    planned: true,
    resumed: false,
    worker_generation: 1,
    at: '2026-09-12T17:00:05.000Z',
    detail: 'os._exit after 400ms',
  }
  const crashScenario: Scenario = {
    id: 'worker-crash',
    title: 'Release 0.2.0 while the harness worker dies mid-write',
    description: '',
    task_prompt: 'Prepare release 0.2.0. Verify before writing again.',
    max_steps: 20,
    fault_kinds: [],
    harness_faults: [{ kind: 'worker_crash', tool: 'write_file', path: 'CHANGELOG.md', nth: 1, after_ms: 400 }],
  }

  function events(): Event[] {
    reset()
    return [
      ev('run.started', { scenario_id: 'worker-crash', model: 'm' }),
      ev('episode.reset', { episode_id: 'ep_c', task_prompt: crashScenario.task_prompt, sandbox_id: 'sb_c' }),
      call(4, 'write_file', { path: 'CHANGELOG.md', content: 'v2' }, 'tu_w'),
      ev('interruption', interruption, 4),
      ev('run.resumed', { worker_generation: 2, resumed_from_event_id: 3, dangling_tool_use_id: 'tu_w', resumed_at: '2026-09-12T17:00:09.000Z' }),
      result(
        4,
        'tu_w',
        'write_file',
        { error: 'harness worker interrupted', code: 'EHARNESS', path: 'CHANGELOG.md' },
        {
          is_error: true,
          outcome: 'unknown',
          error_class: { origin: 'real', layer: 'harness', code: 'EHARNESS', kind: null, label: 'real: harness worker interrupted mid-call (outcome unknown)', outcome_known: false, side_effect_applied: null },
        },
      ),
      call(5, 'read_file', { path: 'CHANGELOG.md' }, 'tu_r'),
      result(5, 'tu_r', 'read_file', { path: 'CHANGELOG.md', content: 'v2', size: 2, sha256: 'x' }),
    ]
  }

  it('reducer: collects the interruption, the resume and the worker generation', () => {
    const s = reduceAll(events())
    expect(s.interruptions).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(s.interruptions[0]!.resumed).toBe(true) // the resume named the dangling call
    expect(s.resumes).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(s.resumes[0]!.worker_generation).toBe(2)
    expect(s.workerGeneration).toBe(2)
    expect(s.status).toBe('running')
  })

  it('verdict: one planned real incident, merged with its EHARNESS result; workers 2 (1 resume)', () => {
    const v = buildVerdict(reduceAll(events()), crashScenario)
    expect(v.incidents).toHaveLength(1)
    const i = v.incidents[0]!
    expect(i).toMatchObject({
      origin: 'real',
      layer: 'harness',
      code: 'EHARNESS',
      planned: true,
      toolUseId: 'tu_w',
      path: 'CHANGELOG.md',
      execution: 'unknown',
      executionSource: 'error_class',
      outcomeKnown: false,
    })
    expect(i.interruption).toEqual({ ...interruption, resumed: true })
    expect(i.errorClass?.code).toBe('EHARNESS')
    expect(v.panels.fault.status).toBe('warn')
    expect(v.panels.fault.headline).toBe('real: harness worker interrupted mid-call (outcome unknown) · step 4 · CHANGELOG.md')
    expect(v.panels.fault.plain).toContain('The scenario deliberately inflicted a real harness worker interruption')
    expect(v.panels.fault.facts.find((f) => f.label === 'planned')!.value).toBe('deliberate real failure')
    expect(v.panels.execution.headline).toBe('Unknown: the worker was interrupted mid-call · ledger pending')
    expect(v.panels.recovery.facts.find((f) => f.label === 'workers')!.value).toBe('2 (1 resume)')
    expect(v.panels.recovery.facts.find((f) => f.label.startsWith('read-back before next write'))!.value).toBe('yes — read back, never rewrote')
    expect(v.sandbox).toMatchObject({ workerGeneration: 2, resumes: 1, lost: null })
    expect(v.panels.task.plain).toContain('includes a real harness interruption (worker crash on write_file CHANGELOG.md)')
    expect(v.panels.task.facts.find((f) => f.label === 'real interruption planned')).toBeTruthy()
  })

  it('the ledger row marked interrupted resolves the write as executed', () => {
    const s = reduceAll([
      ...events(),
      ev('episode.evaluated', {
        episode_id: 'ep_c',
        score: 100,
        passed: true,
        checks: [{ id: 'verified_before_rewrite', ok: true, weight: 2, detail: 'verified at step 5' }],
        tests: { passed: 6, failed: 0, errors: 0, output: '' },
        ledger: [
          { step: 1, ts: '', tool: 'write_file', args_digest: 'a', path: 'CHANGELOG.md', mutating: true, outcome: 'ok', duration_ms: 1, interrupted: true },
          { step: 2, ts: '', tool: 'read_file', args_digest: 'b', path: 'CHANGELOG.md', mutating: false, outcome: 'ok', duration_ms: 1 },
        ],
      }),
      ev('run.finished', { status: 'ok', evaluation_status: 'ok' }),
    ])
    const v = buildVerdict(s, crashScenario)
    const i = v.incidents[0]!
    expect(i.execution).toBe('executed')
    expect(i.executionSource).toBe('ledger')
    expect(i.sideEffectApplied).toBe(true)
    expect(v.panels.execution.headline).toBe('Write landed, worker was interrupted (ledger confirms)')
    expect(v.panels.execution.status).toBe('ok')
    expect(v.panels.recovery.status).toBe('ok')
    expect(v.sandbox.lost).toBe(false)
  })

  it('the RunRecord seeds interruptions and worker_generation when the events were lost', () => {
    reset()
    const rec: RunRecord = {
      run_id: 'r_c',
      status: 'ok',
      scenario_id: 'worker-crash',
      model: 'm',
      max_steps: 20,
      created_at: '2026-09-12T17:00:00.000Z',
      events: [],
      usage: { input_tokens: 0, output_tokens: 0 },
      interruptions: [interruption],
      worker_generation: 2,
    }
    const s = fromRunRecord(rec)
    expect(s.interruptions).toEqual([interruption])
    expect(s.workerGeneration).toBe(2)
    const v = buildVerdict(s, null)
    expect(v.incidents).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(v.incidents[0]!.id).toBe('interruption:tu_w')
    expect(v.incidents[0]!.execution).toBe('unknown')
    expect(v.incidents[0]!.executionSource).toBe('none')
    expect(v.sandbox.resumes).toBe(1)
  })
})

describe('(d) a run with no failures', () => {
  it('is ok on fault and execution, and recovery has nothing to do', () => {
    reset()
    const s = reduceAll([
      ev('run.started', { scenario_id: 'lost-ack', model: 'm' }),
      ev('episode.reset', { episode_id: 'ep_d', task_prompt: 'Do the thing. Then stop.' }),
      call(1, 'read_file', { path: 'README.md' }, 'tu_1'),
      result(1, 'tu_1', 'read_file', { path: 'README.md', content: 'x', size: 1, sha256: 'a' }, { outcome: 'executed' }),
      call(2, 'run_command', { command: 'python -m pytest -q' }, 'tu_2'),
      result(2, 'tu_2', 'run_command', { stdout: '6 passed', stderr: '', exit_code: 0, duration_ms: 3 }, { outcome: 'executed' }),
      ev('episode.evaluated', {
        episode_id: 'ep_d',
        score: 100,
        passed: true,
        checks: [{ id: 'version_bumped', ok: true, weight: 1, detail: '' }],
        tests: { passed: 6, failed: 0, errors: 0, output: '' },
        ledger: [],
      }),
      ev('run.finished', { status: 'ok' }),
    ])
    const v = buildVerdict(s, null)
    expect(v.incidents).toEqual([])
    expect(v.panels.fault.status).toBe('ok')
    expect(v.panels.fault.headline).toBe('No failures')
    expect(v.panels.execution.status).toBe('ok')
    expect(v.panels.execution.headline).toBe('Every call was acknowledged')
    expect(v.panels.recovery.status).toBe('ok')
    expect(v.panels.recovery.headline).toBe('Nothing to recover from')
    expect(v.panels.result.status).toBe('ok')
    expect(v.panels.result.headline).toBe('Score 100 · tests 6/6 · checks 1/1')
    expect(v.sandbox.lost).toBe(false)
    expect(v.diff).toBeNull()
  })

  it('while live with no failures yet, fault is ok and recovery/result are pending', () => {
    reset()
    const s = reduceAll([ev('run.started', { scenario_id: 'lost-ack', model: 'm' })])
    const v = buildVerdict(s, null)
    expect(v.live).toBe(true)
    expect(v.panels.fault.status).toBe('ok')
    expect(v.panels.execution.headline).toBe('Every call so far was acknowledged')
    expect(v.panels.recovery.status).toBe('pending')
    expect(v.panels.result.status).toBe('pending')
    expect(v.panels.task.status).toBe('unknown')
  })
})

describe('(e) message text is never consulted', () => {
  function run(output: unknown): Event[] {
    reset()
    return [
      ev('run.started', { scenario_id: 'lost-ack', model: 'm' }),
      call(1, 'read_file', { path: 'CHANGELOG.md' }, 'tu_1'),
      result(1, 'tu_1', 'read_file', output, { is_error: true }),
      ev('run.finished', { status: 'error' }),
    ]
  }

  it('an error result without error_class has unknown origin whatever its message says', () => {
    const scary = buildVerdict(reduceAll(run({ error: 'sandbox terminated: the Modal sandbox is gone', code: 'ESANDBOX' })), null)
    const bland = buildVerdict(reduceAll(run({ error: 'something else entirely', code: 'ESANDBOX' })), null)
    expect(scary.incidents).toHaveLength(1)
    const i = scary.incidents[0]!
    expect(i.origin).toBe('unknown')
    expect(i.layer).toBeNull()
    expect(i.label).toBeNull()
    expect(i.errorClass).toBeNull()
    expect(i.execution).toBe('unknown')
    expect(i.executionSource).toBe('none')
    expect(scary.panels.fault.status).toBe('unknown')
    expect(scary.panels.fault.plain).toContain('predates error classification')
    expect(scary.panels.execution.plain).toContain('predates error classification')
    expect(scary.sandbox.lost).toBeNull()
    // identical verdicts: the message text changed nothing, and no raw payload carries it
    expect(scary).toEqual(bland)
    expect(JSON.stringify(scary)).not.toContain('Modal sandbox is gone')
  })

  it('a plain-text error body (not even JSON) is treated the same way', () => {
    const v = buildVerdict(reduceAll(run('sandbox terminated')), null)
    expect(v.incidents[0]).toMatchObject({ origin: 'unknown', code: null, execution: 'unknown', executionSource: 'none' })
  })

  it('a non-error result with a non-zero exit code on a faulted call is "failed (exit code)" only without ledger or class', () => {
    reset()
    const events = [
      call(1, 'run_command', { command: 'cat README.md' }, 'tu_1'),
      result(1, 'tu_1', 'run_command', { stdout: '', stderr: 'cat: README.md: No such file or directory', exit_code: 1, duration_ms: 2 }, {
        fault: { step: 1, kind: 'missing_file', path: 'README.md', mode: 'transient' },
      }),
      ev('run.finished', { status: 'ok' }),
    ]
    const before = buildVerdict(reduceAll(events), null)
    expect(before.incidents[0]).toMatchObject({ origin: 'injected', kind: 'missing_file', code: 'ENOENT', execution: 'failed', executionSource: 'exit_code' })
    expect(before.panels.execution.headline).toBe('Command ran and failed (from exit code)')

    const after = buildVerdict(
      reduceAll([
        ...events.slice(0, 2),
        ev('episode.evaluated', {
          episode_id: 'ep',
          score: 0,
          passed: false,
          checks: [],
          tests: { passed: 0, failed: 1, errors: 0, output: '' },
          ledger: [
            { step: 1, ts: '', tool: 'run_command', args_digest: 'a', command: 'cat README.md', mutating: false, fault: { step: 1, kind: 'missing_file', path: 'README.md', mode: 'transient' }, outcome: 'short_circuit', exit_code: 1, duration_ms: 1, origin: 'injected', error_code: 'ENOENT' },
          ],
        }),
        events[2]!,
      ]),
      null,
    )
    expect(after.incidents[0]).toMatchObject({ execution: 'not_executed', executionSource: 'ledger', sideEffectApplied: false })
    expect(after.panels.execution.headline).toBe('Nothing ran (answered at the boundary; file still on disk, ledger confirms)')
  })
})

describe('helpers', () => {
  it('summariseDiff counts +/- lines and skips file headers', () => {
    expect(summariseDiff([])).toBeNull()
    expect(
      summariseDiff([
        { path: 'a', unified: '--- a/a\n+++ b/a\n@@ -1 +1,2 @@\n-x\n+y\n+z\n' },
        { path: 'b', unified: '--- a/b\n+++ b/b\n@@ -1 +1 @@\n+only\n' },
      ]),
    ).toEqual({ files: 2, added: 3, removed: 1, paths: ['a', 'b'] })
  })

  it('collectIncidents returns the same public incidents as buildVerdict', () => {
    const s = fromRunRecord(record)
    expect(collectIncidents(s)).toEqual(buildVerdict(s, null).incidents)
    expect(Object.keys(collectIncidents(s)[0]!).some((k) => k.startsWith('__'))).toBe(false)
  })

  it('initialState carries the new fields', () => {
    expect(initialState()).toMatchObject({
      interruptions: [],
      resumes: [],
      workerGeneration: 1,
      errorClass: null,
      scenario: null,
      sandboxEvents: [],
      sandboxId: null,
      sandboxAlive: null,
    })
  })
})

  // execution; a simulated refusal is not (that was the experiment).
  const realFailed = incidents.some((i) => (i.execution === 'failed' || i.execution === 'not_executed') && i.origin === 'real')

old = """    // the lost ack never got a ledger: it stays unknown and says why
    const ack = v.incidents[1]!

    expect(v.panels.execution.status).toBe('unknown')

    expect(v.panels.execution.status).toBe('fail')
"""

import { fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

import { TooltipProvider } from '@/components/ui/tooltip'

import { SandboxChip, VerdictStrip } from './VerdictStrip'
import { ackLostRecovered, allUnknown, sandboxLost } from './test-fixtures'

function renderStrip(ui: React.ReactElement) {
  return render(<TooltipProvider>{ui}</TooltipProvider>)
}

function panel(key: string): HTMLElement {
  return document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-slot="verdict-panel"][data-key="${key}"]`)!
}

function openDetails(key: string): HTMLElement {
  const label = key.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + key.slice(1)
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: `${label} details` }))
  return screen.getByRole('region', { name: `${label} details` })
}

function factChips(key: string): string[] {
  return Array.from(panel(key).querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="verdict-fact"]')).map((el) => el.textContent ?? '')
}

describe('VerdictStrip', () => {
  it('renders nothing without a verdict', () => {
    const { container } = renderStrip(<VerdictStrip verdict={null} />)
    expect(container.querySelector('[data-slot="verdict-strip"]')).toBeNull()
  })

  it('reads an injected ack_lost run that recovered and scored 100', () => {
    renderStrip(<VerdictStrip verdict={ackLostRecovered} taskPrompt="Release 0.2.0 …" />)

    const strip = screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Run verdict' })
    expect(strip.getAttribute('data-status')).toBe('ok')
    expect(within(strip).getAllByRole('article')).toHaveLength(5)

    // the five headlines and their status glyphs
    expect(screen.getByText('Release 0.2.0: changelog entry and version bump')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByText('Simulated lost ack on CHANGELOG.md at step 2')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByText('The write executed — the ledger confirms it landed')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByText('Read back before writing again; sandbox survived')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByText('Score 100 · tests 8/8 · checks 3/3')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(panel('task')).getByRole('img', { name: 'ok' })).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(panel('fault')).getByRole('img', { name: 'warning' })).toBeTruthy()
    expect(panel('fault').getAttribute('data-status')).toBe('warn')
    expect(within(panel('result')).getByRole('img', { name: 'ok' })).toBeTruthy()

    // fact chips render "label: value" with the model's tone
    expect(factChips('fault')).toEqual(['origin:simulated', 'layer:boundary', 'code:ETIMEDOUT'])
    const originChip = panel('fault').querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="verdict-fact"]')!
    expect(originChip.getAttribute('data-tone')).toBe('warn')
    expect(factChips('result')).toEqual(['score:100', 'tests:8/8', 'checks:3/3'])

    // fault details: origin badge + verbatim taxonomy label + dictionary text for the code
    const fault = openDetails('fault')
    const badge = fault.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="origin-badge"]')!
    expect(badge.textContent).toBe('simulated')
    expect(badge.getAttribute('data-origin')).toBe('injected')
    expect(within(fault).getByText('simulated: lost ack (write landed; response withheld)')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(fault).getByText('step 2 · Write · CHANGELOG.md')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(fault).getByText('ETIMEDOUT', { selector: 'code' })).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(fault).getByText(/response never arrived/)).toBeTruthy() // dictionary text, not the raw message
    expect(within(fault).getByText('The write was applied and the acknowledgement withheld.')).toBeTruthy()

    // execution details: "executed", sourced from the ledger, the write had landed, verify hint
    const execution = openDetails('execution')
    const row = execution.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="verdict-execution"]')!
    expect(row.getAttribute('data-outcome')).toBe('executed')
    expect(within(row).getByText('executed')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(row).getByText('from the ledger after grading')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(row).getByText('side effect applied — the write had landed')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(row).getByText(/verify before retrying/)).toBeTruthy()

    // recovery details: the read-back follow-up and the sandbox summary
    const recovery = openDetails('recovery')
    const followUp = recovery.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="verdict-follow-up"]')!
    expect(followUp.getAttribute('data-kind')).toBe('read')
    expect(within(followUp).getByRole('img', { name: 'pass' })).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(followUp).getByText('step 3 · read-back · Read · ok')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(recovery).getByText('survived — the run was graded inside it')).toBeTruthy()

    // result details: diff paths, tests, every check with its description
    const result = openDetails('result')
    expect(within(result).getByText('+7')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(result).getByText('−0')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(result).getByText('src/ratelimiter/version.py')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(result).getByText(/tests 8 passed · 0 failed · 0 errors/)).toBeTruthy()
    const checks = result.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="verdict-check"]')
    expect(checks).toHaveLength(3)
    expect(within(checks[0]!).getByRole('img', { name: 'pass' })).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(checks[0]!).getByText(/read CHANGELOG.md before writing it again/)).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(checks[0]!).getByText('read_file CHANGELOG.md at step 3')).toBeTruthy()

    // task details: the full prompt passed in
    const task = openDetails('task')
    expect(within(task).getByText('Release 0.2.0 …')).toBeTruthy()
  })

  it('reads a real sandbox loss as a failure with grading blocked', () => {
    renderStrip(<VerdictStrip verdict={sandboxLost} />)

    expect(screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Run verdict' }).getAttribute('data-status')).toBe('interrupted')
    expect(panel('fault').getAttribute('data-status')).toBe('fail')
    expect(within(panel('fault')).getByRole('img', { name: 'failed' })).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(panel('result')).getByRole('img', { name: 'failed' })).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByText('Real sandbox loss at step 5')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByText('Not graded — sandbox unavailable')).toBeTruthy()

    const fault = openDetails('fault')
    const badge = fault.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="origin-badge"]')!
    expect(badge.textContent).toBe('real')
    expect(badge.getAttribute('data-origin')).toBe('real')
    expect(within(fault).getByText('real: sandbox terminated or unavailable')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(fault).getByText('no — unplanned')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(fault).getByText('ESANDBOX', { selector: 'code' })).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(fault).getByText(/sandbox unavailable/)).toBeTruthy()

    const execution = openDetails('execution')
    const row = execution.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="verdict-execution"]')!
    expect(row.getAttribute('data-outcome')).toBe('not_executed')
    expect(within(row).getByText('not executed')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(row).getByText('from error class')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(row).getByText('no side effect — nothing changed on disk')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(row).getByText('harness attempts: 3')).toBeTruthy()

    const recovery = openDetails('recovery')
    expect(within(recovery).getByText('no later call touched this path')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(recovery).getByText('no worker resumed the run')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(recovery).getByText('lost at step 5')).toBeTruthy()

    const result = openDetails('result')
    expect(within(result).getByText('No workspace diff was recorded.')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(result).getByText('grading skipped')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(result).getByText('Not graded — real: sandbox terminated or unavailable')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(result.querySelectorAll('[data-slot="verdict-check"]')).toHaveLength(0)
  })
