Both gates are clean: tsc exit 0, vitest 17 files / 180 tests passed (the original 146 plus my 25 and the UI agent's 9 — their two earlier VerdictStrip failures were against their own hand-built fixtures and have since been resolved on their side). Nothing further to request.

What was done

Files created: src/lib/reliability.ts, src/lib/reliability.test.ts, src/lib/codes.ts Files edited (additive): src/lib/types.ts, src/lib/reducer.ts, src/lib/checks.ts, src/lib/format.ts, src/lib/api.ts (only EVENT_TYPES += interruption, run.resumed, episode.sandbox). Nothing under src/components or src/pages was touched; reliability.types.ts was left as the coordinator extended it.

Exported symbols

reliability.ts - buildVerdict(state: ViewState, scenario: Scenario | null, opts?: { live?: boolean }): Verdict — pure; live defaults to !isTerminal(state.status); scenario falls back to state.scenario (from episode.reset.data.scenario, which the lost-ack capture carries). - collectIncidents(state, opts?): Incident[], summariseDiff(diffs): DiffSummary | null.

codes.ts: CODE_TEXT: Record<ErrorCode, {title, plain, verifyHint?}>, codeText(code), exitCodeText(n), originText(o), originPlain(o), layerText(l), taxonomyLabel(origin, kind, layer) (the taxonomy's fixed (origin,kind)/(real,layer) → label table), codeForInjectedKind(kind).

reducer.ts (additive): ToolResultView.{errorClass, outcome, attempts, sandbox}; ViewState.{interruptions, resumes, workerGeneration, errorClass, scenario, sandboxEvents, sandboxId, sandboxAlive, resetAttempt, evaluationStatus, evaluationError}; new asErrorClass(v) export; handles interruption, run.resumed (marks the dangling interruption resumed), episode.sandbox; fromRunRecord maps error_class / interruptions / worker_generation; TERMINAL_STATUSES += unevaluated, interrupted. parseToolResult's signature is unchanged, so transcript.ts and the component fixtures compile untouched.

types.ts: all 11 ErrorCodes + ERROR_CODES, ErrorOrigin/ErrorLayer (+ const arrays), ErrorClass, Interruption, HarnessFaultKind/HarnessFault, CheckSpec, FaultPublic, Scenario.{harness_faults?, checks?, faults_public?}, FaultFired.{origin?, layer?, description?}, LedgerEntry.{origin?, error_code?, interrupted?}, ToolOutcome, ToolResultData.{outcome?, error_class?, error_code?, attempts?, sandbox?}, RunFinishedData.{error_class?, evaluation_status?, evaluation_error?, steps?, score?}, RunResumedData, EpisodeSandboxData, EpisodeResetData.{scenario?, sandbox_id?, attempt?}, RunRecord.{error_class?, interruptions?, worker_generation?}, RunStatus/EventType widened.

checks.ts: RECOVERY_CHECK_IDS, isRecoveryCheck(id). format.ts: firstSentence(s, max), plural(n, word, words?).

Headline / plain wording settled on

Panel State → headline
task scenario title → id → scenarioId → Unknown task; plain = first sentence of the prompt + "The scenario injects " / "Includes a real harness interruption (worker crash on write_file CHANGELOG.md)" + "Graded on: …" (scenario.checks preferred, checkText fallback)
fault none No failures; one <label> · step N · <path>; many N failures: k simulated, s staged, m real, u unknown (non-zero only). Status fail / unknown / warn / ok
execution Write landed, acknowledgement lost (ledger confirms) · Write landed, worker was interrupted (ledger confirms) · Nothing was written (denied at the boundary[, ledger confirms]) · Nothing ran (answered at the boundary; file still on disk…) / (sandbox unavailable) · Command ran and failed (from exit code) · Unknown: the worker was interrupted mid-call[ · ledger pending] / Unknown: the acknowledgement was lost / Unknown: this record predates error classification · many: 2 not executed · 1 unknown · none: Every call [so far] was acknowledged
recovery Recovery verified by the grader (k/k checks) · Recovery check failed: <id>[ +n] · Nothing to recover from · live: Awaiting grading · read-back so far: yes/no/n.a. · Grader did not score recovery for this scenario · Not graded: <label>. Facts: read-back (live hint), harness attempts, workers 2 (1 resume), sandbox survived / lost at step N / unknown
result Score 100 · tests 8/8 · checks 3/3 · 2 files changed · Not graded: real: sandbox terminated or unavailable · Awaiting grading · N files changed so far

Judgment calls to note

  • Execution priority: tool.result.data.outcome (≠ unknown) → error_class (known outcome; a filesystem-layer class means the sandbox really ran it → failed) → ledger (ack_lost/interrupted+ok → executed, short_circuit → not executed, error → failed/not executed by code) → exit code → unknown with the reason spelled out.
  • Deviation from the literal spec: execution panel is fail for a real not_executed as well as a real failed (a real ESANDBOX stopping a call from running should not read as ok).
  • I wrote tests 8/8 rather than hidden tests 8/8 — GRADING.md runs visible + hidden.
  • Incident.step is the harness turn (matches ToolCallView.step); the demo's FaultFired.step (6) is the ledger step and is what the ledger match uses.
  • A run-level error_class nothing else carries (e.g. EGYM at evaluate) becomes its own incident so the fault panel shows it; blockedBy falls back to evaluation_error when no label exists.
  • harness_faults/checks/faults_public are optional on Scenario so the pre-taxonomy capture still type-checks.
  • The test "message text never consulted" asserts two runs differing only in error text produce deep-equal verdicts and that the text appears nowhere in the serialised verdict (raw refs omit output).
Index
